```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/sumeet/matrix/elijah/.claude/worktrees/spi-243-pr1 && gh pr create --draft --base dev --title \"Cross-FS: Carve out packages/auth (SPI-243 PR-1)\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## What this is\n\nThis is the first of two PRs that together do SPI-243. It does only the unglamorous part: pick up the existing `services/api/auth.py` and move it into a brand-new `packages/auth/` package, so the rest of the codebase imports from one well-defined location.\n\nThere's no behavior change. The exact same code does the exact same thing — it just lives in a different folder, with the registry data separated from the FastAPI-specific bits. All existing auth tests pass without modification.\n\nThe point of doing this as its own PR is that the *next* PR adds real new behavior (Cloudflare Zero Trust header bridging, admin flag, per-user run cap), and we want the file-move noise out of the way so reviewers can focus on what's actually changing.\n\n## Technical detail\n\n### Summary\n\n- New package: `packages/auth/`\n- Split into two submodules:\n  - `registry.py` — `User`, `UserRegistry`, `load_user_registry`, the private TOML loader\n  - `dependencies.py` — `get_current_user`, `get_user_registry`, the FastAPI `APIKeyHeader`\n- Public surface re-exported via `packages/auth/__init__.py`\n- `services/api/auth.py` deleted\n- Two import sites updated: `services/api/app.py` (line 106) and `tests/test_api_auth.py` (line 16)\n\n### Approach\n\nPure mechanical move. Git detected the rename from `services/api/auth.py` → `packages/auth/registry.py` (65% similarity). The 35% difference is the FastAPI dependency code (`get_current_user`, `get_user_registry`, `_api_key_header`) that was split out into `dependencies.py`. Everything else is byte-identical to its previous location.\n\nThe decision to split rather than just rename came from the auth plan (`docs/plans/auth-plan.md`, decision #17): the package is intended to keep public surface and internals separable. Registering this shape now avoids relitigating it in PR-2 once new code lands.\n\nSource artifact: `docs/plans/auth-plan.md` (#266, draft).\n\n### Tests\n\nNo new tests. The existing `tests/test_api_auth.py` (24 tests covering registry primitives, TOML loading, fallback mode, and the FastAPI dependency behavior) passes unchanged after updating its single import line.\n\n### Verification\n\n- [x] `uv run ruff check .` — clean\n- [x] `uv run ruff format --check .` — 552 files already formatted\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest --ignore=tests/frontend -x -q` — 1355 passed, 8 skipped\n  - One transient failure on `test_research_driver_pipeline_parallel.py::test_parallel_questions_faster_than_sequential` during the first run, which is a wall-clock-timing test (asserts parallel execution < 0.6s; got 1.01s under load). Re-ran the file in isolation and all 11 tests pass cleanly. The auth refactor cannot affect parallel question-runner timing.\n- [x] `tests/test_api_auth.py` runs green on its own (24 passed)\n\n### Risks\n\nLow. Pure refactor. The only call sites are two files and both have been updated. Git rename detection caught the move, so the diff is small and reviewable.\n\nThe split into two submodules is the one non-mechanical choice. If reviewers prefer a single `packages/auth/auth.py` file instead, that's a one-commit revert and I'll do it before merge.\n\n### Branch-merge\n\nBranches off `dev` at `53888a0`. Targets `dev`. No stacked dependencies — PR-2 will branch off this once it lands, or off the same `dev` base if we'd rather have them independent.\n\n### Out of scope (deliberately)\n\n- CF ZT header bridging — SPI-243 PR-2\n- `admin: bool` and `max_runs: int` on `User` — SPI-243 PR-2\n- `[defaults]` TOML section + auto-provisioning unknown CF emails — SPI-243 PR-2\n- Removing the shared `ELIJAH_API_KEY` fallback — SPI-247 (full-auth epic)\n- Wiring `get_current_user` into the actual route gates — today routes use `services/api/dependencies.require_api_key` (the legacy single-key path). Per-user identity is loaded but not yet enforced. That wiring lands as part of SPI-243 PR-2 or SPI-247, not here.\n\nSource plan: #266\n\ncc @kungfusaini\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Open draft PR-1 against dev"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"User registry: data model and TOML loader.\n\nDefines the ``User`` record, the ``UserRegistry`` lookup container, and the\n``load_user_registry`` entry point. Supports two identity paths into the\nregistry: ``X-API-Key`` (programmatic callers) and email (browser callers\nwho hit the service through Cloudflare Zero Trust).\n\nSchema, expressed as TOML:\n\n    [defaults]\n    max_runs = 3              # auto-provision default for unknown CF emails\n\n    [[users]]\n    user_id  = \"sumeet\"\n    email    = \"sumeet@sumeetsaini.com\"\n    api_key  = \"...\"\n    admin    = true           # default false; admins bypass the run cap\n    # max_runs absent → uncapped (typical for admins)\n\n    [[users]]\n    user_id  = \"alice\"\n    email    = \"alice@example.com\"\n    api_key  = \"...\"\n    max_runs = 5              # explicit per-user cap\n\nBackward-compat: when no ``config/api_users.toml`` is present, the registry\nfalls back to ``ELIJAH_API_KEY`` (single anonymous user, no email). Existing\nsingle-key deployments keep working unchanged.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport secrets\nimport tomllib\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_DEFAULT_USERS_PATH = Path(\"config/api_users.toml\")\n_USERS_PATH_ENV = \"ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass User:\n    user_id: str\n    name: str\n    role: str = \"operator\"\n    email: str | None = None\n    is_admin: bool = False\n    max_runs: int | None = None\n    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass RegistryDefaults:\n    \"\"\"Values used to auto-provision unknown CF-authenticated emails.\"\"\"\n\n    max_runs: int | None = None\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass UserRegistry:\n    by_api_key: dict[str, User]\n    by_email: dict[str, User]\n    defaults: RegistryDefaults\n    fallback_mode: bool\n\n    @property\n    def is_empty(self) -> bool:\n        return not self.by_api_key and not self.by_email\n\n    @property\n    def n_users(self) -> int:\n        return len({user.user_id for user in self.by_api_key.values()} | {user.user_id for user in self.by_email.values()})\n\n    def lookup(self, api_key: str) -> User | None:\n        if not api_key:\n            return None\n        for stored_key, user in self.by_api_key.items():\n            try:\n                if secrets.compare_digest(stored_key, api_key):\n                    return user\n            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n                continue\n        return None\n\n    def lookup_by_email(self, email: str) -> User | None:\n        if not email:\n            return None\n        return self.by_email.get(email.strip().lower())\n\n    def provision_for_email(self, email: str) -> User:\n        \"\"\"Synthesize a non-admin ``User`` for a CF-authenticated email not in\n        the registry. Uses ``defaults.max_runs`` as the run cap.\"\"\"\n        normalized = email.strip().lower()\n        return User(\n            user_id=normalized,\n            name=normalized,\n            email=normalized,\n            is_admin=False,\n            max_runs=self.defaults.max_runs,\n        )\n\n\ndef load_user_registry(\n    *,\n    users_path: Path | None = None,\n    fallback_api_key: str | None = None,\n    fallback_user_id: str = \"shared\",\n    fallback_user_name: str = \"shared\",\n) -> UserRegistry:\n    candidate_path: Path | None = users_path\n    if candidate_path is None:\n        env_path = os.environ.get(_USERS_PATH_ENV, \"\").strip()\n        if env_path:\n            candidate_path = Path(env_path)\n    if candidate_path is None:\n        if _DEFAULT_USERS_PATH.exists():\n            candidate_path = _DEFAULT_USERS_PATH\n\n    if candidate_path is not None and candidate_path.exists():\n        try:\n            return _load_from_toml(candidate_path)\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — operator-facing config error\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Failed to load api_users from %s (%s: %s); falling back to ELIJAH_API_KEY.\",\n                candidate_path,\n                type(exc).__name__,\n                exc,\n            )\n\n    if fallback_api_key:\n        return UserRegistry(\n            by_api_key={\n                fallback_api_key: User(\n                    user_id=fallback_user_id,\n                    name=fallback_user_name,\n                    role=\"operator\",\n                )\n            },\n            by_email={},\n            defaults=RegistryDefaults(),\n            fallback_mode=True,\n        )\n\n    return UserRegistry(\n        by_api_key={},\n        by_email={},\n        defaults=RegistryDefaults(),\n        fallback_mode=True,\n    )\n\n\ndef _parse_defaults(payload: dict[str, Any], path: Path) -> RegistryDefaults:\n    raw = payload.get(\"defaults\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: 'defaults' must be a table\")\n    max_runs = raw.get(\"max_runs\")\n    if max_runs is not None and not isinstance(max_runs, int):\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: defaults.max_runs must be an int\")\n    return RegistryDefaults(max_runs=max_runs)\n\n\ndef _load_from_toml(path: Path) -> UserRegistry:\n    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n        payload = tomllib.load(f)\n    defaults = _parse_defaults(payload, path)\n    raw_users = payload.get(\"users\") or []\n    if not isinstance(raw_users, list):\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: 'users' must be a list of tables\")\n    by_api_key: dict[str, User] = {}\n    by_email: dict[str, User] = {}\n    seen_user_ids: set[str] = set()\n    for idx, entry in enumerate(raw_users):\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: users[{idx}] must be a table\")\n        api_key = str(entry.get(\"api_key\") or \"\").strip()\n        user_id = str(entry.get(\"user_id\") or \"\").strip()\n        name = str(entry.get(\"name\") or user_id).strip()\n        role = str(entry.get(\"role\") or \"operator\").strip()\n        email_raw = entry.get(\"email\")\n        email = str(email_raw).strip().lower() if email_raw else None\n        admin_raw = entry.get(\"admin\", False)\n        if not isinstance(admin_raw, bool):\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: users[{idx}].admin must be a bool\")\n        max_runs_raw = entry.get(\"max_runs\")\n        if max_runs_raw is not None and not isinstance(max_runs_raw, int):\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: users[{idx}].max_runs must be an int\")\n        metadata_raw = entry.get(\"metadata\") or {}\n        if not api_key:\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: users[{idx}] missing 'api_key'\")\n        if not user_id:\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: users[{idx}] missing 'user_id'\")\n        if api_key in by_api_key:\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: duplicate api_key in users[{idx}] (user_id={user_id})\")\n        if user_id in seen_user_ids:\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: duplicate user_id={user_id} in users[{idx}]\")\n        if email and email in by_email:\n            raise ValueError(f\"{path}: duplicate email={email} in users[{idx}]\")\n        seen_user_ids.add(user_id)\n        metadata: dict[str, Any] = dict(metadata_raw) if isinstance(metadata_raw, dict) else {}\n        user = User(\n            user_id=user_id,\n            name=name,\n            role=role,\n            email=email,\n            is_admin=admin_raw,\n            max_runs=max_runs_raw,\n            metadata=metadata,\n        )\n        by_api_key[api_key] = user\n        if email:\n            by_email[email] = user\n    return UserRegistry(\n        by_api_key=by_api_key,\n        by_email=by_email,\n        defaults=defaults,\n        fallback_mode=False,\n    )\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"RegistryDefaults\",\n    \"User\",\n    \"UserRegistry\",\n    \"load_user_registry\",\n]\n"
}

```
1	"""FastAPI dependencies for resolving the caller to a ``User``.
2	
3	Reads the X-API-Key header, looks the key up in the ``UserRegistry``
4	attached to ``request.app.state.user_registry``, and attaches the
5	resolved ``User`` to ``request.state.user`` for downstream layers.
6	"""
7	
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, Security, status
11	from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
12	
13	from .registry import User, UserRegistry
14	
15	_api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name="X-API-Key", auto_error=False)
16	
17	
18	def get_user_registry(request: Request) -> UserRegistry:
19	    registry: UserRegistry | None = getattr(request.app.state, "user_registry", None)
20	    if registry is None:
21	        return UserRegistry(by_api_key={}, fallback_mode=True)
22	    return registry
23	
24	
25	def get_current_user(
26	    request: Request,
27	    supplied_key: str | None = Security(_api_key_header),
28	) -> User | None:
29	    registry = get_user_registry(request)
30	    if registry.is_empty:
31	        return None
32	
33	    if not supplied_key:
34	        raise HTTPException(
35	            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
36	            detail={"code": "unauthorized", "message": "Missing API key."},
37	        )
38	
39	    user = registry.lookup(supplied_key)
40	    if user is None:
41	        raise HTTPException(
42	            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
43	            detail={"code": "unauthorized", "message": "Invalid API key."},
44	        )
45	
46	    request.state.user = user
47	    return user
48	
49	
50	__all__ = [
51	    "get_current_user",
52	    "get_user_registry",
53	]
54

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/elijah/.claude/worktrees/spi-243-pr2/packages/auth/dependencies.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"FastAPI dependencies for resolving the caller to a ``User``.\n\nTwo identity paths are supported, in priority order:\n\n1. ``Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email`` — injected by Cloudflare Zero\n   Trust on every authenticated browser request. Looked up by email in\n   the registry; unknown emails are auto-provisioned with defaults.\n2. ``X-API-Key`` — for programmatic callers. Looked up against the\n   registered API keys.\n\nThe resolved ``User`` is attached to ``request.state.user`` so downstream\nlayers (job submission, filtering, run-cap enforcement) can scope on it.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException, Request, Security, status\nfrom fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader\n\nfrom .registry import RegistryDefaults, User, UserRegistry\n\nCF_AUTHENTICATED_EMAIL_HEADER = \"Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email\"\n\n_api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name=\"X-API-Key\", auto_error=False)\n\n\ndef get_user_registry(request: Request) -> UserRegistry:\n    registry: UserRegistry | None = getattr(request.app.state, \"user_registry\", None)\n    if registry is None:\n        return UserRegistry(\n            by_api_key={},\n            by_email={},\n            defaults=RegistryDefaults(),\n            fallback_mode=True,\n        )\n    return registry\n\n\ndef get_current_user(\n    request: Request,\n    supplied_key: str | None = Security(_api_key_header),\n) -> User | None:\n    registry = get_user_registry(request)\n    if registry.is_empty:\n        return None\n\n    cf_email = request.headers.get(CF_AUTHENTICATED_EMAIL_HEADER)\n    if cf_email:\n        user = registry.lookup_by_email(cf_email) or registry.provision_for_email(cf_email)\n        request.state.user = user\n        return user\n\n    if not supplied_key:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,\n            detail={\"code\": \"unauthorized\", \"message\": \"Missing API key.\"},\n        )\n\n    user = registry.lookup(supplied_key)\n    if user is None:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,\n            detail={\"code\": \"unauthorized\", \"message\": \"Invalid API key.\"},\n        )\n\n    request.state.user = user\n    return user\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"CF_AUTHENTICATED_EMAIL_HEADER\",\n    \"get_current_user\",\n    \"get_user_registry\",\n]\n"
}
```

{
  "command": "cd /Users/sumeet/matrix/elijah/.claude/worktrees/spi-243-pr2 && gh pr create --draft --base sumeet/spi-243-pr1-auth-package --title \"Cross-FS: Bridge CF ZT email to per-user identity (SPI-243 PR-2)\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## What this is\n\nThis PR is the actual SPI-243 feature: it gives every browser user a per-user identity at the API layer by reading the email Cloudflare Zero Trust already injects on every request. Today, every GUI user looks identical to the backend because they all share one API key. After this PR, a job submitted by Sumeet is distinguishable from a job submitted by Patrick is distinguishable from a job submitted by anyone else — and the system knows which of them are admins.\n\nThe identity comes from a header CF ZT puts on the request: `Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email`. The backend reads it, looks the email up in `api_users.toml`, and resolves to a `User` object. If the email isn't in the TOML, the backend auto-provisions a non-admin user with a default run cap, so onboarding a demo user is just \"let them through CF ZT\" — no TOML edit required.\n\nThis unblocks the rest of the demo-auth epic: SPI-246 stamps `submitted_by` on jobs and filters the GUI list per user, and the new run-cap ticket enforces a per-user cap of 3 counted jobs. Both rely on the `User` object this PR puts on `request.state.user`.\n\n## Technical detail\n\nStacked on PR #267 (SPI-243 PR-1 — the `packages/auth/` carve-out). Base branch for review: `sumeet/spi-243-pr1-auth-package`.\n\n### Summary\n\n- New header path in `get_current_user`: reads `Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email`, looks the email up in the registry, auto-provisions if unknown.\n- `User` gains three new fields: `email`, `is_admin`, `max_runs`.\n- `UserRegistry` gains a `by_email` index, a `defaults` field, a `lookup_by_email` method, and a `provision_for_email` factory.\n- `api_users.toml` schema extended: `[defaults]` top-level table; `email`, `admin`, `max_runs` per entry. All additions are optional and backward-compat — existing entries keep working.\n- `config/api_users.example.toml` added as the canonical template.\n\n### Approach\n\nCF header takes precedence over `X-API-Key` when both are present. Rationale: GUI traffic always carries the CF header; the frontend may also inject the shared `ELIJAH_API_KEY` today (which would resolve to the anonymous \"shared\" user). CF identity is the more specific signal, so it wins.\n\nAuto-provisioning produces a synthetic non-admin `User` with `user_id = email`, `max_runs = registry.defaults.max_runs`. Missing `[defaults]` section means uncapped, which is intentionally permissive — the run-cap ticket is the place that decides what to do with `max_runs is None`.\n\nPer-user `max_runs` semantics (from auth plan decision #12): a user entry with no `max_runs` field is uncapped. Auto-provisioned users use the `[defaults].max_runs` value. Admins are uncapped by virtue of having no `max_runs` field — no separate code path required.\n\nAll `UserRegistry` fields now have safe defaults, so test code constructing minimal registries with positional arguments (e.g. `UserRegistry(by_api_key={\"sk\": user})`) keeps compiling without test churn.\n\nSource plan: PR #266.\n\n### Tests\n\n19 new tests across three classes; all 43 in the file pass.\n\n`TestLoadUserRegistrySchemaExtensions` (10 tests):\n- `admin: true` parsed; absent admin defaults to false.\n- `max_runs` parsed as int; absent means `None` (uncapped).\n- `email` lowercased + trimmed on load, case-insensitive on lookup.\n- Duplicate email across entries falls back with a clear log message.\n- `admin` must be a bool; `max_runs` must be an int (per-entry and in `[defaults]`); both fall back with operator-facing log messages.\n- `[defaults]` table parsed; absent means `RegistryDefaults()` with `max_runs=None`.\n\n`TestProvisionForEmail` (3 tests):\n- Auto-provisioned user picks up `defaults.max_runs` as the cap.\n- Email is normalized before being used as `user_id` and `email`.\n- Empty `RegistryDefaults` produces uncapped auto-provisioned users.\n\n`TestGetCurrentUserCFHeaderPath` (5 tests):\n- CF header with a registered email returns that `User` (with `is_admin`).\n- CF header with an unknown email auto-provisions a non-admin with the default cap.\n- CF header takes precedence over `X-API-Key` when both are present.\n- CF header email lookup is case-insensitive.\n- `X-API-Key` path still works unchanged when no CF header is present.\n\n### Verification\n\n- [x] `uv run ruff check .` — clean\n- [x] `uv run ruff format --check .` — 552 files already formatted\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest --ignore=tests/frontend -x -q` — 1374 passed, 8 skipped\n  - One transient failure on `test_research_driver_pipeline_parallel.py::test_parallel_questions_faster_than_sequential` (same wall-clock-timing flake observed in PR-1, asserts parallel < 0.6s, got 0.89s under load). Re-ran the file in isolation and all 11 tests pass cleanly. Auth changes cannot affect parallel question-runner timing.\n- [x] `tests/test_api_auth.py` runs green on its own (43 passed)\n\n### Risks\n\nLow-to-medium.\n\n- The CF header path is purely additive. The existing `X-API-Key` path is unchanged when no CF header is present, so callers that don't go through CF ZT see no behavior change.\n- CF-vs-API-key precedence is a judgment call. If the GUI relied on the shared `ELIJAH_API_KEY` being interpreted as the anonymous user even when CF was identifying the same caller, that path now resolves to the CF identity instead. This is the desired behavior for the demo, but worth being aware of.\n- Schema additions are all backward-compat; existing `api_users.toml` files without `email`/`admin`/`max_runs`/`[defaults]` load fine.\n- The CF header is currently *trusted* (no JWT verification). The auth plan calls out that this is safe as long as the CF tunnel is the only ingress that injects the header in the demo deployment. JWT verification is a follow-up in the full-auth epic.\n\n### Branch-merge\n\nStacked on PR #267 (`sumeet/spi-243-pr1-auth-package`). Should not merge until PR-1 lands. After PR-1 merges into `dev`, this branch should be rebased onto fresh `dev` to drop the PR-1 commit from its history.\n\n### Out of scope (deliberately)\n\n- Wiring `get_current_user` into the actual route gates. Routes today gate on `services/api/dependencies.require_api_key` (the legacy single-key path). Per-user identity is loaded into `request.state.user` but no route enforces it yet. The route-wiring move lives with SPI-246 (`submitted_by` plus filtering) or SPI-247 (removing the shared-key fallback), whichever lands first.\n- `submitted_by` on the jobs table — SPI-246.\n- Run-cap enforcement at submit time — new run-cap ticket.\n- LiteLLM per-user identity — SPI-244 (full-auth epic).\n- JWT verification of the CF Access token — new full-auth ticket.\n\nSource plan: #266\nStacked on: #267\n\ncc @kungfusaini\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Open draft PR-2 against PR-1 branch"
}

waiitt why do we have api_key=[REDACTED]" I thought we werer not using the keys like that?

> AGENT

Good catch — that's a real inconsistency. The plan is "CF email is the primary identity for GUI users; API key is for programmatic callers only" — so admins like Sumeet and Patrick shouldn't need API keys at all. They get identified by email through CF, period.

But PR-2's TOML loader still requires `api_key` per entry (legacy from before SPI-243). That's wrong now. Let me fix it on PR-2 before going further on SPI-246.

The right schema:
- `api_key` becomes optional
- `email` becomes optional
- At least one of the two must be present (otherwise the entry is unreachable)
- Admins typically have email only; programmatic-only callers have api_key only; some may have both

Switching back to the PR-2 worktree to fix:

172	    raw_users = payload.get("users") or []

> DEVELOPER

I dont want seperate api keys fir now plesae or do we need ti rigjt now?

> AGENT

Honest answer: **no, we don't need per-user API keys for the demo.**

Reasoning:
- All GUI users come through CF ZT — they're identified by email, no key involved
- Non-CF callers (CI scripts, frontend's legacy shared-key injection, tests) all use the single `ELIJAH_API_KEY` env var via `require_api_key`. That's the shared-key fallback that doesn't go away until SPI-247.
- Per-user X-API-Key paths exist in code from SPI-236 but nothing actually uses them in the demo deployment.

So I'll:
1. Keep the loader's behavior I just changed (`api_key` now *accepted but not required*; at least one of `api_key`/`email` per entry).
2. Strip `api_key` out of the example TOML so it doesn't suggest a setup we don't want.
3. Fix the existing `test_missing_api_key_raises` test since the rule changed.

The per-user `api_key` field stays *accepted by the schema* (so we don't have to do a schema migration if anyone ever adds it) but it's invisible in the example. Email-only entries are the documented shape.

The real file is gitignored;\n# only this example template ships with the repo.\n#\n# Identity for GUI users comes from Cloudflare Zero Trust — the tunnel\n# injects Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email on every authenticated\n# request, and the backend looks the email up here.\n#\n# Programmatic callers (CI scripts, healthchecks) authenticate via the\n# single shared ELIJAH_API_KEY env var, not per-user keys. Per-user\n# api_key support exists in the loader for future use but is not used\n# by the demo and is intentionally not shown here.\n\n# Auto-provision defaults. Applied when a CF-authenticated email is not\n# in the [[users]] list — the user is synthesized as a non-admin with\n# these caps.\n[defaults]\nmax_runs = 3\n\n# Admin entries. Admins are uncapped (no max_runs field) and see all\n# jobs in the GUI automatically. Identity is by email only.\n[[users]]\nuser_id = \"sumeet\"\nname    = \"Sumeet\"\nemail   = \"sumeet@sumeetsaini.com\"\nadmin   = true\n\n[[users]]\nuser_id = \"patrick\"\nname    = \"Patrick\"\nemail   = \"patrick.a.m2020@gmail.com\"\nadmin   = true\n\n# Non-admin entry with a custom cap that overrides [defaults].max_runs.\n# Omit max_runs to make this user uncapped.\n#\n# [[users]]\n# user_id  = \"alice\"\n# name     = \"Alice\"\n# email    = \"alice@example.com\"\n# max_runs = 10\n"
}

1	"""
2	Database-backed job service for API submission, polling, and artifact access.
3	"""
4	
5	from __future__ import annotations
6	
7	import logging
8	import uuid
9	from collections.abc import Callable
10	from dataclasses import dataclass
11	from datetime import UTC, datetime
12	from pathlib import Path
13	from typing import Any
14	
15	from core.run_config import create_run_research_run_config, load_run_research_run_config
16	from packages.contracts import (
17	    ApiError,
18	    JobAcceptedResponse,
19	    JobProgress,
20	    JobRequestContext,
21	    JobResultSummary,
22	    JobState,
23	    JobStatusResponse,
24	    JobType,
25	    ResearchRunMode,
26	)

28	    DatabaseJobQueue,
29	    WorkerStorageAdapters,
30	    resolve_database_job_queue,
31	    resolve_worker_storage_adapters,
32	)

36	type ResearchJobRunner = Callable[..., dict[str, Any]]
37	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
38	
39	
40	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
41	class ResolvedArtifact:
42	    artifact_id: str
43	    path: Path
44	    content_type: str
45	    label: str
46	    kind: str
47	
48	
49	class ArtifactBytesMissingError(RuntimeError):
50	    def __init__(self, *, artifact_id: str, object_key: str) -> None:
51	        super().__init__(f"Artifact bytes missing for {artifact_id}: {object_key}")
52	        self.artifact_id = artifact_id
53	        self.object_key = object_key
54	
55	
56	def _format_timestamp(value: datetime | None) -> str | None:
57	    if value is None:
58	        return None
59	    if value.tzinfo is None:
60	        value = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
61	    return value.astimezone(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
62	
63	
64	def _build_run_id(*, prefix: str = "api") -> str:
65	    timestamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
66	    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
67	    return f"{timestamp}_{prefix}_{suffix}"
68	
69	
70	class JobService:
71	    def __init__(
72	        self,
73	        *,
74	        config: ApiConfig | None = None,
75	        storage_adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters | None = None,
76	        research_job_runner: ResearchJobRunner | None = None,
77	        job_queue: DatabaseJobQueue | None = None,
78	    ) -> None:
79	        self._config = config or ApiConfig()
80	        self._storage_adapters = resolve_worker_storage_adapters(
81	            storage_adapters,
82	            runtime_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
83	        )
84	        self._job_queue = job_queue or resolve_database_job_queue(self._config.storage_runtime)
85	        self._research_job_runner = research_job_runner
86	        self._artifact_cache_root = self._config.storage_runtime.local_workspace_root / "artifact_cache"
87	        self._artifact_cache_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
88	
89	    @property
90	    def config(self) -> ApiConfig:
91	        return self._config
92	
93	    @property
94	    def storage_adapters(self) -> WorkerStorageAdapters:
95	        return self._storage_adapters
96	
97	    @property
98	    def job_queue(self) -> DatabaseJobQueue:
99	        return self._job_queue
100	
101	    def submit_research_job(
102	        self,
103	        *,
104	        question_ids: list[str],
105	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
106	        graph_id: str | None = None,
107	        mode: ResearchRunMode | None = None,
108	        model_set: str | None = None,
109	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
110	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
111	        return self._submit_job(
112	            question_ids=question_ids,
113	            graph_id=graph_id,
114	            request_context=JobRequestContext(
115	                mode=mode or ResearchRunMode.FULL_RUN,
116	                run_config_id=(run_config_id or "").strip() or None,
117	                model_set=(model_set or "").strip() or None,
118	            ),
119	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
120	        )
121	
122	    def submit_research_step_job(
123	        self,
124	        *,
125	        question_id: str,
126	        step_id: str,
127	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
128	        graph_id: str | None = None,
129	        filter_mode: str | None = None,
130	        model_set: str | None = None,
131	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
132	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
133	        return self._submit_job(
134	            question_ids=[question_id],
135	            graph_id=graph_id,
136	            request_context=JobRequestContext(
137	                mode=ResearchRunMode.SINGLE_STEP,
138	                question_id=(question_id or "").strip() or None,
139	                step_id=(step_id or "").strip() or None,
140	                filter_mode=(filter_mode or "").strip() or None,
141	                run_config_id=(run_config_id or "").strip() or None,
142	                model_set=(model_set or "").strip() or None,
143	            ),
144	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
145	        )
146	
147	    def submit_research_batch_step_job(
148	        self,
149	        *,
150	        question_ids: list[str],
151	        step_id: str,
152	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
153	        graph_id: str | None = None,
154	        model_set: str | None = None,
155	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
156	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
157	        return self._submit_job(
158	            question_ids=list(question_ids),
159	            graph_id=graph_id,
160	            request_context=JobRequestContext(
161	                mode=ResearchRunMode.BATCH_STEP,
162	                step_id=(step_id or "").strip() or None,
163	                run_config_id=(run_config_id or "").strip() or None,
164	                model_set=(model_set or "").strip() or None,
165	            ),
166	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
167	        )
168	
169	    def _submit_job(
170	        self,
171	        *,
172	        question_ids: list[str],
173	        graph_id: str | None,
174	        request_context: JobRequestContext,
175	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
176	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
177	        resolved_graph_id = (graph_id or self._config.default_graph_id).strip() or self._config.default_graph_id
178	        resolved_run_config = self._resolve_run_config_for_submission(request_context=request_context)
179	        resolved_model_set = str((resolved_run_config.llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip() or None
180	        resolved_request_context = request_context.model_copy(
181	            update={
182	                "run_config_id": resolved_run_config.run_config_id,
183	                "run_config_hash": resolved_run_config.config_hash,
184	                "model_set": resolved_model_set,
185	            }
186	        )
187	
188	        # Idempotency: if a key is provided and a non-failed job with the
189	        # same key already exists, return that job instead of creating a new one.
190	        clean_key = (idempotency_key or "").strip() or None
191	        if clean_key:
192	            existing = self._job_queue.find_by_idempotency_key(clean_key)
193	            if existing is not None and str(existing.state or "") not in ("failed",):
194	                try:
195	                    state = JobState(str(existing.state or JobState.QUEUED.value))
196	                except ValueError:
197	                    state = JobState.QUEUED
198	                return JobAcceptedResponse(
199	                    job_id=existing.job_id,
200	                    job_type=JobType.RESEARCH,
201	                    state=state,
202	                    status_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{existing.job_id}",
203	                    result_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{existing.job_id}/result",
204	                    request_context=resolved_request_context,
205	                )
206	
207	        run_id = _build_run_id(prefix="api")
208	        job_id = self._job_queue.submit_job(
209	            job_type=JobType.RESEARCH.value,
210	            graph_id=resolved_graph_id,
211	            run_id=run_id,
212	            question_ids=list(question_ids),
213	            request_data=resolved_request_context.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True),
214	            run_config_id=resolved_run_config.run_config_id,
215	            idempotency_key=clean_key,
216	        )
217	        return JobAcceptedResponse(
218	            job_id=job_id,
219	            job_type=JobType.RESEARCH,
220	            state=JobState.QUEUED,
221	            status_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{job_id}",
222	            result_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{job_id}/result",
223	            request_context=resolved_request_context,
224	        )
225	
226	    def _resolve_run_config_for_submission(self, *, request_context: JobRequestContext):
227	        explicit_run_config_id = (request_context.run_config_id or "").strip()
228	        compatibility_model_set = (request_context.model_set or "").strip()
229	        if explicit_run_config_id and compatibility_model_set:
230	            stored = load_run_research_run_config(
231	                run_config_id=explicit_run_config_id,
232	                storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
233	            )
234	            stored_model_set = str((stored.llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip()
235	            if stored_model_set and stored_model_set != compatibility_model_set:
236	                raise ValueError("run_config_id cannot be combined with a different model_set")
237	            return stored
238	        if explicit_run_config_id:
239	            return load_run_research_run_config(
240	                run_config_id=explicit_run_config_id,
241	                storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
242	            )
243	        return create_run_research_run_config(
244	            storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
245	            cli_overrides={"model_set": compatibility_model_set} if compatibility_model_set else {},
246	            label="api-default" if not compatibility_model_set else f"api-{compatibility_model_set}",
247	            description="Default API run config created for a submission without an explicit run_config_id.",
248	            source_kind="api_default",
249	            created_by="api",
250	        )
251	
252	    def get_status(self, job_id: str) -> JobStatusResponse | None:
253	        row = self._job_queue.get_job(job_id)
254	        if row is None:
255	            return None
256	        try:
257	            state = JobState(str(row.state or JobState.FAILED.value))
258	        except ValueError:
259	            state = JobState.FAILED
260	        try:
261	            job_type = JobType(str(row.job_type or JobType.RESEARCH.value))
262	        except ValueError:
263	            job_type = JobType.RESEARCH
264	
265	        result_payload = row.result_data if isinstance(row.result_data, dict) else None
266	        result = None
267	        if result_payload is not None:
268	            try:
269	                result = JobResultSummary.model_validate(result_payload)
270	            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
271	                result = None
272	
273	        request_context = None
274	        if isinstance(row.request_data, dict):
275	            try:
276	                request_context = JobRequestContext.model_validate(row.request_data)
277	            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
278	                request_context = None
279	
280	        error = None
281	        if row.error_code or row.error_message:
282	            error = ApiError(
283	                code=str(row.error_code or "job_failed"),
284	                message=str(row.error_message or "Job failed."),
285	            )
286	
287	        return JobStatusResponse(
288	            job_id=row.job_id,
289	            job_type=job_type,
290	            state=state,
291	            submitted_at=_format_timestamp(row.submitted_at) or "",
292	            run_id=str(row.run_id or "").strip() or None,
293	            graph_id=str(row.graph_id or "").strip(),
294	            requested_question_ids=list(row.requested_question_ids or []),
295	            request_context=request_context,
296	            progress=JobProgress(
297	                processed_count=int(row.processed_count or 0),
298	                total_count=int(row.total_count or 0),
299	                current_question_id=str(row.current_question_id or "").strip() or None,
300	                current_step=str(row.current_step or "").strip() or None,
301	                started_at=_format_timestamp(row.started_at),
302	                finished_at=_format_timestamp(row.finished_at),
303	            ),
304	            result=result,
305	            error=error,
306	        )
307	
308	    def cancel_job(self, job_id: str) -> str:
309	        """Cancel a job. Returns 'cancelled', 'already_terminal', or 'not_found'."""
310	        return self._job_queue.cancel_job(job_id)
311	
312	    def get_result(self, job_id: str) -> JobResultSummary | None:
313	        status = self.get_status(job_id)
314	        if status is None:
315	            return None
316	        return status.result
317	
318	    def resolve_artifact(self, artifact_id: str) -> ResolvedArtifact | None:
319	        artifact = self._job_queue.get_artifact(artifact_id)
320	        if artifact is None:
321	            return None
322	        downloader = getattr(self._storage_adapters.artifact_repository, "download_object_to_local", None)
323	        if not callable(downloader):
324	            return None
325	        file_name = Path(artifact.object_key).name or artifact.artifact_id
326	        target_path = self._artifact_cache_root / artifact.artifact_id / file_name
327	        try:
328	            path = downloader(object_key=artifact.object_key, target_path=target_path)
329	        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
330	            logger.warning(
331	                "Artifact metadata points at missing bytes",
332	                extra={"artifact_id": artifact.artifact_id, "object_key": artifact.object_key},
333	                exc_info=True,
334	            )
335	            raise ArtifactBytesMissingError(artifact_id=artifact.artifact_id, object_key=artifact.object_key) from exc
336	        return ResolvedArtifact(
337	            artifact_id=artifact.artifact_id,
338	            path=Path(path),
339	            content_type=artifact.content_type,
340	            label=artifact.label,
341	            kind=artifact.kind,
342	        )
343	
344	    def shutdown(self) -> None:
345	        return None
346	
347	
348	__all__ = ["ArtifactBytesMissingError", "JobService", "ResearchJobRunner", "ResolvedArtifact"]
349

1	"""
2	Job submission and polling routes for the API service.
3	"""
4	
5	from __future__ import annotations
6	
7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status
8	
9	from core.research_driver.question_workflow import step_id_is_valid
10	from packages.contracts import (
11	    ApiError,
12	    JobAcceptedResponse,
13	    JobResultSummary,
14	    JobStatusResponse,
15	    ResearchJobSubmitRequest,
16	    ResearchStepJobSubmitRequest,
17	)
18	from packages.storage import GraphStorePaths, WorkerStorageAdapters
19	from services.api.rate_limit import limiter
20	
21	from ..dependencies import get_graph_store_paths, get_job_service, get_storage_adapters
22	from ..job_service import JobService
23	
24	router = APIRouter()
25	
26	
27	@router.post("/jobs/research", response_model=JobAcceptedResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED)
28	@limiter.limit("5/minute")
29	def submit_research_job(
30	    request: Request,
31	    body: ResearchJobSubmitRequest,
32	    job_service: JobService = Depends(get_job_service),
33	    storage_adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters = Depends(get_storage_adapters),
34	    graph_store_paths: GraphStorePaths = Depends(get_graph_store_paths),
35	) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
36	    existing_ids = {
37	        question.question_id for question in storage_adapters.graph_store_repository.load_questions(graph_store_paths.questions_path)
38	    }
39	    missing = sorted(set(body.question_ids) - existing_ids)
40	    if missing:
41	        raise HTTPException(
42	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
43	            detail=ApiError(
44	                code="questions_not_found",
45	                message=f"Unknown question ids: {', '.join(missing)}",
46	                details={"missing_question_ids": missing},
47	            ).model_dump(),
48	        )
49	    try:
50	        return job_service.submit_research_job(
51	            question_ids=body.question_ids,
52	            run_config_id=body.run_config_id,
53	            graph_id=body.graph_id,
54	            mode=body.mode,
55	        )
56	    except ValueError as exc:
57	        raise HTTPException(
58	            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
59	            detail=ApiError(code="invalid_run_config", message=str(exc)).model_dump(),
60	        ) from exc
61	
62	
63	@router.post("/jobs/research-steps", response_model=JobAcceptedResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED)
64	@limiter.limit("10/minute")

71	) -> JobAcceptedResponse:

121	        results.append(

130	                    "current_question_id": str(row.current_question_id or "").strip() or None,

133	                },

139	@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/cancel")
140	def cancel_job(
141	    job_id: str,
142	    job_service: JobService = Depends(get_job_service),
143	) -> dict:
144	    result = job_service.cancel_job(job_id)
145	    if result == "not_found":
146	        raise HTTPException(
147	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
148	            detail=ApiError(code="job_not_found", message=f"Unknown job: {job_id}").model_dump(),
149	        )
150	    if result == "already_terminal":
151	        raise HTTPException(
152	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
153	            detail=ApiError(
154	                code="job_already_terminal",
155	                message=f"Job '{job_id}' is already in a terminal state.",
156	            ).model_dump(),
157	        )
158	    return {"job_id": job_id, "status": "cancelled"}
159	
160

162	def get_job_status(
163	    job_id: str,
164	    job_service: JobService = Depends(get_job_service),
165	) -> JobStatusResponse:
166	    payload = job_service.get_status(job_id)
167	    if payload is None:
168	        raise HTTPException(
169	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
170	            detail=ApiError(code="job_not_found", message=f"Unknown job: {job_id}").model_dump(),
171	        )
172	    return payload
173	
174	
175	@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}/result", response_model=JobResultSummary)
176	def get_job_result(
177	    job_id: str,
178	    job_service: JobService = Depends(get_job_service),
179	) -> JobResultSummary:
180	    status_payload = job_service.get_status(job_id)
181	    if status_payload is None:
182	        raise HTTPException(
183	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
184	            detail=ApiError(code="job_not_found", message=f"Unknown job: {job_id}").model_dump(),
185	        )
186	    if status_payload.result is None:
187	        raise HTTPException(
188	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
189	            detail=ApiError(
190	                code="job_result_not_ready",
191	                message=f"Job '{job_id}' has not finished with a result yet.",
192	            ).model_dump(),
193	        )
194	    return status_payload.result
195	
196	
197	__all__ = ["router"]

1	"""
2	Persistent job queue and artifact registry backed by the storage database.
3	"""
4	
5	from __future__ import annotations
6	
7	import uuid
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from datetime import timedelta
10	from pathlib import Path
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	from sqlalchemy import or_, select
14	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
15	
16	from .database import ArtifactRow, JobEventRow, JobRow, RunRow, RunStatus, StorageDatabase, ensure_database_schema, utc_now
17	from .interfaces import ArtifactRepository
18	from .runtime_config import StorageRuntimeConfig, resolve_storage_runtime_config
19	
20	
21	@dataclass(frozen=True)
22	class ClaimedJob:
23	    job_id: str
24	    job_type: str
25	    graph_id: str
26	    run_id: str
27	    run_config_id: str | None
28	    requested_question_ids: list[str]
29	    request_data: dict[str, Any]
30	
31	
32	@dataclass(frozen=True)
33	class StoredArtifact:
34	    artifact_id: str
35	    job_id: str | None
36	    question_id: str | None
37	    run_id: str | None
38	    kind: str
39	    label: str
40	    content_type: str
41	    object_key: str
42	    size_bytes: int
43	    sha256: str
44	
45	
46	class DatabaseJobQueue:
47	    def __init__(self, *, config: StorageRuntimeConfig, database: StorageDatabase | None = None) -> None:
48	        self._config = resolve_storage_runtime_config(config)
49	        self._database = database or ensure_database_schema(self._config)
50	
51	    def submit_job(
52	        self,
53	        *,
54	        job_type: str,
55	        graph_id: str,
56	        run_id: str,
57	        question_ids: list[str],
58	        request_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
59	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
60	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
61	    ) -> str:
62	        job_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
63	        now = utc_now()
64	        effective_request_data = dict(request_data or {})
65	        if run_config_id:
66	            effective_request_data["run_config_id"] = run_config_id
67	        row = JobRow(
68	            job_id=job_id,
69	            job_type=job_type,
70	            state="queued",
71	            graph_id=graph_id,
72	            run_id=run_id,
73	            run_config_id=run_config_id,
74	            requested_question_ids=list(question_ids),
75	            request_data=effective_request_data or None,
76	            processed_count=0,
77	            total_count=len(question_ids),
78	            submitted_at=now,
79	            created_at=now,
80	            updated_at=now,
81	        )
82	        if idempotency_key:
83	            row.idempotency_key = idempotency_key
84	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
85	            session.add(row)
86	        return job_id
87	
88	    def find_by_idempotency_key(self, key: str) -> JobRow | None:
89	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
90	            return session.scalars(
91	                select(JobRow).where(JobRow.idempotency_key == key).order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.desc()).limit(1)
92	            ).first()
93	
94	    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> JobRow | None:
95	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
96	            return session.get(JobRow, job_id)
97	
98	    def list_active_jobs(self, *, limit: int = 10) -> list[JobRow]:
99	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
100	            return list(
101	                session.scalars(
102	                    select(JobRow).where(JobRow.state.in_(["queued", "running"])).order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.asc()).limit(limit)
103	                ).all()
104	            )
105	
106	    def claim_next_job(self, *, worker_name: str | None = None) -> ClaimedJob | None:
107	        owner = str(worker_name or self._config.queue_worker_name).strip() or "elijah-worker"
108	        now = utc_now()
109	        lease_expires = now + timedelta(seconds=max(1, int(self._config.queue_lease_seconds)))
110	        orphan_cutoff = now - timedelta(seconds=2 * max(1, int(self._config.queue_lease_seconds)))
111	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
112	            stmt = (
113	                select(JobRow)
114	                .where(
115	                    or_(
116	                        JobRow.state == "queued",
117	                        (JobRow.state == "running") & (JobRow.lease_expires_at.is_not(None)) & (JobRow.lease_expires_at <= now),
118	                        # Reclaim orphaned jobs with NULL lease that have been running too long
119	                        (JobRow.state == "running")
120	                        & (JobRow.lease_expires_at.is_(None))
121	                        & (JobRow.started_at.is_not(None))
122	                        & (JobRow.started_at <= orphan_cutoff),
123	                    )
124	                )
125	                .order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.asc())
126	            )
127	            if self._database.dialect_name() != "sqlite":
128	                stmt = stmt.with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
129	            row = session.scalars(stmt.limit(1)).first()
130	            if row is None:
131	                return None
132	            row.state = "running"
133	            row.started_at = row.started_at or now
134	            row.lease_owner = owner
135	            row.lease_expires_at = lease_expires
136	            row.updated_at = now
137	            session.add(
138	                JobEventRow(
139	                    job_id=row.job_id,
140	                    event_type="claimed",
141	                    payload={"worker_name": owner},
142	                    created_at=now,
143	                )
144	            )
145	            return ClaimedJob(
146	                job_id=row.job_id,
147	                job_type=row.job_type,
148	                graph_id=row.graph_id,
149	                run_id=row.run_id,
150	                run_config_id=row.run_config_id,
151	                requested_question_ids=list(row.requested_question_ids or []),
152	                request_data=dict(row.request_data or {}),
153	            )
154	
155	    def update_progress(
156	        self,
157	        *,
158	        job_id: str,
159	        processed_count: int,
160	        total_count: int,
161	        current_question_id: str | None,
162	        current_step: str | None,
163	        state: str | None = None,
164	        event_type: str = "progress",
165	        payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
166	    ) -> None:
167	        now = utc_now()
168	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
169	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
170	            if row is None:
171	                raise KeyError(job_id)
172	            row.processed_count = int(processed_count)
173	            row.total_count = int(total_count)
174	            row.current_question_id = str(current_question_id or "").strip() or None
175	            row.current_step = str(current_step or "").strip() or None
176	            row.state = str(state or row.state)
177	            row.lease_expires_at = now + timedelta(seconds=max(1, int(self._config.queue_lease_seconds)))
178	            row.updated_at = now
179	            session.add(
180	                JobEventRow(
181	                    job_id=job_id,
182	                    event_type=event_type,
183	                    payload=dict(payload or {}),
184	                    created_at=now,
185	                )
186	            )
187	
188	    def complete_job(
189	        self,
190	        *,
191	        job_id: str,
192	        state: str,
193	        result_data: dict[str, Any] | None,
194	        error_code: str | None = None,
195	        error_message: str | None = None,
196	    ) -> None:
197	        now = utc_now()
198	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
199	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
200	            if row is None:
201	                raise KeyError(job_id)
202	            row.state = str(state)
203	            row.result_data = dict(result_data or {}) if result_data is not None else None
204	            row.error_code = str(error_code or "").strip() or None
205	            row.error_message = str(error_message or "").strip() or None
206	            row.finished_at = now
207	            row.current_question_id = None
208	            row.current_step = None
209	            row.lease_owner = None
210	            row.lease_expires_at = None
211	            row.updated_at = now
212	            session.add(
213	                JobEventRow(
214	                    job_id=job_id,
215	                    event_type="completed" if state in {"succeeded", "partial"} else ("cancelled" if state == "cancelled" else "failed"),
216	                    payload={
217	                        "state": state,
218	                        "error_code": row.error_code,
219	                        "error_message": row.error_message,
220	                    },
221	                    created_at=now,
222	                )
223	            )
224	            # SPI-219: Sync RunRow.status so API-submitted runs don't stay stuck
225	            # in RUNNING. The _ensure_run_row helper (called during artifact
226	            # registration) creates the RunRow with RUNNING, but nothing on the
227	            # API job path transitioned it to a terminal state.
228	            _JOB_STATE_TO_RUN_STATUS = {
229	                "succeeded": RunStatus.COMPLETED,
230	                "partial": RunStatus.COMPLETED,
231	                "failed": RunStatus.FAILED,
232	                "cancelled": RunStatus.CANCELLED,
233	            }
234	            run_status = _JOB_STATE_TO_RUN_STATUS.get(state)
235	            if run_status and row.run_id:
236	                run_row = session.get(RunRow, row.run_id)
237	                if run_row is not None:
238	                    run_row.status = run_status
239	                    run_row.finished_at = now
240	                    if row.run_config_id is not None:
241	                        run_row.run_config_id = row.run_config_id
242	
243	    def cancel_job(self, job_id: str) -> str:
244	        """Cancel a job. Returns 'cancelled', 'already_terminal', or 'not_found'."""
245	        terminal_states = {"succeeded", "partial", "failed", "cancelled"}
246	        now = utc_now()
247	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
248	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
249	            if row is None:
250	                return "not_found"
251	            if str(row.state or "") in terminal_states:
252	                return "already_terminal"
253	            row.state = "cancelled"
254	            row.finished_at = now
255	            row.current_question_id = None
256	            row.current_step = None
257	            row.lease_owner = None
258	            row.lease_expires_at = None
259	            row.error_code = "operator_cancelled"
260	            row.error_message = "Job cancelled by user."
261	            row.updated_at = now
262	            session.add(
263	                JobEventRow(
264	                    job_id=job_id,
265	                    event_type="cancelled",
266	                    payload={"state": "cancelled"},
267	                    created_at=now,
268	                )
269	            )
270	        return "cancelled"
271	
272	    def is_cancelled(self, job_id: str) -> bool:
273	        """Check if a job has been cancelled."""
274	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
275	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
276	            return row is not None and str(row.state or "") == "cancelled"
277	
278	    def _ensure_run_row(
279	        self,
280	        session: Session,
281	        *,
282	        run_id: str | None,
283	        question_id: str | None,
284	        job_id: str | None,
285	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
286	    ) -> None:
287	        # ArtifactRow.run_id has an FK to runs.run_id, but submit_job only writes a
288	        # JobRow — nothing on the API submission path populates the runs table. Without
289	        # this helper, every run_summary_json write fails with artifacts_run_id_fkey.
290	        # Resolution order for question_id (required by runs_question_id_fkey):
291	        # explicit arg → JobRow.requested_question_ids[0] → give up and let the
292	        # downstream INSERT fail loudly with the original FK error.
293	        if not run_id:
294	            return
295	        resolved_run_config_id = (run_config_id or "").strip() or None
296	        if not resolved_run_config_id and job_id:
297	            job = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
298	            if job is not None:
299	                resolved_run_config_id = job.run_config_id
300	        existing_run = session.get(RunRow, run_id)
301	        if existing_run is not None:
302	            if resolved_run_config_id is not None and existing_run.run_config_id is None:
303	                existing_run.run_config_id = resolved_run_config_id
304	            return
305	
306	        resolved_qid = (question_id or "").strip()
307	        if not resolved_qid and job_id:
308	            job = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
309	            if job is not None:
310	                resolved_run_config_id = resolved_run_config_id or job.run_config_id
311	                requested = job.requested_question_ids or []
312	                if requested:
313	                    resolved_qid = str(requested[0]).strip()
314	        if not resolved_qid:
315	            return
316	
317	        session.add(
318	            RunRow(
319	                run_id=run_id,
320	                question_id=resolved_qid,
321	                job_id=job_id,
322	                run_config_id=resolved_run_config_id,
323	                status=RunStatus.RUNNING,
324	            )
325	        )
326	        session.flush()
327	
328	    def register_artifact(
329	        self,
330	        *,
331	        job_id: str | None,
332	        question_id: str | None,
333	        run_id: str | None,
334	        kind: str,
335	        label: str,
336	        content_type: str,
337	        object_key: str,
338	        size_bytes: int,
339	        sha256: str,
340	    ) -> StoredArtifact:
341	        artifact_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
342	        now = utc_now()
343	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
344	            self._ensure_run_row(session, run_id=run_id, question_id=question_id, job_id=job_id)
345	            row = ArtifactRow(
346	                artifact_id=artifact_id,
347	                job_id=job_id,
348	                question_id=question_id,
349	                run_id=run_id,
350	                kind=kind,
351	                label=label,
352	                content_type=content_type,
353	                object_key=object_key,
354	                size_bytes=int(size_bytes),
355	                sha256=sha256,
356	                created_at=now,
357	            )
358	            session.add(row)
359	        return StoredArtifact(
360	            artifact_id=artifact_id,
361	            job_id=job_id,
362	            question_id=question_id,
363	            run_id=run_id,
364	            kind=kind,
365	            label=label,
366	            content_type=content_type,
367	            object_key=object_key,
368	            size_bytes=int(size_bytes),
369	            sha256=sha256,
370	        )
371	
372	    def store_and_register_artifact(
373	        self,
374	        *,
375	        artifact_repository: ArtifactRepository,
376	        path: Path,
377	        job_id: str | None,
378	        question_id: str | None,
379	        run_id: str | None,
380	        kind: str,
381	        label: str,
382	        content_type: str,
383	    ) -> StoredArtifact:
384	        uploaded = artifact_repository.ensure_object_copy(Path(path))
385	        return self.register_artifact(
386	            job_id=job_id,
387	            question_id=question_id,
388	            run_id=run_id,
389	            kind=kind,
390	            label=label,
391	            content_type=content_type,
392	            object_key=uploaded.object_key,
393	            size_bytes=uploaded.size_bytes,
394	            sha256=uploaded.sha256,
395	        )
396	
397	    def get_artifact(self, artifact_id: str) -> StoredArtifact | None:
398	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
399	            row = session.get(ArtifactRow, artifact_id)
400	            if row is None:
401	                return None
402	            return StoredArtifact(
403	                artifact_id=row.artifact_id,
404	                job_id=row.job_id,
405	                question_id=row.question_id,
406	                run_id=row.run_id,
407	                kind=row.kind,
408	                label=row.label,
409	                content_type=row.content_type,
410	                object_key=row.object_key,
411	                size_bytes=row.size_bytes,
412	                sha256=row.sha256,
413	            )
414	
415	
416	def resolve_database_job_queue(
417	    config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
418	) -> DatabaseJobQueue:
419	    resolved = resolve_storage_runtime_config(config)
420	    database = ensure_database_schema(resolved)
421	    return DatabaseJobQueue(config=resolved, database=database)
422	
423	
424	__all__ = ["ClaimedJob", "DatabaseJobQueue", "StoredArtifact", "resolve_database_job_queue"]
425

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/elijah/.claude/worktrees/spi-246/packages/storage/job_queue.py",
  "old_string": "    def list_active_jobs(self, *, limit: int = 10) -> list[JobRow]:\n        with self._database.session_scope() as session:\n            return list(\n                session.scalars(\n                    select(JobRow).where(JobRow.state.in_([\"queued\", \"running\"])).order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.asc()).limit(limit)\n                ).all()\n            )",
  "new_string": "    def list_active_jobs(self, *, limit: int = 10, submitted_by: str | None = None) -> list[JobRow]:\n        with self._database.session_scope() as session:\n            stmt = select(JobRow).where(JobRow.state.in_([\"queued\", \"running\"]))\n            if submitted_by is not None:\n                stmt = stmt.where(JobRow.submitted_by == submitted_by)\n            stmt = stmt.order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.asc()).limit(limit)\n            return list(session.scalars(stmt).all())\n\n    def list_jobs(self, *, limit: int = 50, submitted_by: str | None = None) -> list[JobRow]:\n        \"\"\"Recent jobs, optionally filtered by submitter. ``submitted_by=None``\n        returns the unfiltered list (admin view); a concrete user_id returns\n        only that user's jobs.\"\"\"\n        with self._database.session_scope() as session:\n            stmt = select(JobRow)\n            if submitted_by is not None:\n                stmt = stmt.where(JobRow.submitted_by == submitted_by)\n            stmt = stmt.order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.desc()).limit(limit)\n            return list(session.scalars(stmt).all())"
}

Returns 'cancelled', 'already_terminal', or 'not_found'.\"\"\"\n        return self._job_queue.cancel_job(job_id)\n\n    def list_jobs(\n        self,\n        *,\n        submitted_by: str | None = None,\n        limit: int = 50,\n    ) -> list[JobStatusResponse]:\n        \"\"\"Recent jobs as ``JobStatusResponse`` summaries. ``submitted_by=None``\n        returns the unfiltered list (admin view); a concrete user_id returns\n        only that user's jobs.\"\"\"\n        rows = self._job_queue.list_jobs(submitted_by=submitted_by, limit=limit)\n        summaries: list[JobStatusResponse] = []\n        for row in rows:\n            status = self.get_status(row.job_id)\n            if status is not None:\n                summaries.append(status)\n        return summaries"

  "old_string": "\"\"\"\nJob submission and polling routes for the API service.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status\n\nfrom core.research_driver.question_workflow import step_id_is_valid\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    ApiError,\n    JobAcceptedResponse,\n    JobResultSummary,\n    JobStatusResponse,\n    ResearchJobSubmitRequest,\n    ResearchStepJobSubmitRequest,\n)\nfrom packages.storage import GraphStorePaths, WorkerStorageAdapters\nfrom services.api.rate_limit import limiter\n\nfrom ..dependencies import get_graph_store_paths, get_job_service, get_storage_adapters\nfrom ..job_service import JobService\n\nrouter = APIRouter()",
  "new_string": "\"\"\"\nJob submission and polling routes for the API service.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status\n\nfrom core.research_driver.question_workflow import step_id_is_valid\nfrom packages.auth import User, get_current_user\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    ApiError,\n    JobAcceptedResponse,\n    JobResultSummary,\n    JobStatusResponse,\n    ResearchJobSubmitRequest,\n    ResearchStepJobSubmitRequest,\n)\nfrom packages.storage import GraphStorePaths, WorkerStorageAdapters\nfrom services.api.rate_limit import limiter\n\nfrom ..dependencies import get_graph_store_paths, get_job_service, get_storage_adapters\nfrom ..job_service import JobService\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\n\ndef _submitter_id(user: User | None) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"User.user_id for stamping submitted_by, or None if no identity resolved.\n\n    Anonymous calls (auth disabled in dev mode) leave submitted_by as None;\n    the column is nullable so the row still inserts cleanly.\"\"\"\n    if user is None:\n        return None\n    return user.user_id\n\n\ndef _list_filter_for(user: User | None) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"submitted_by filter for list endpoints. Admins (and anonymous callers\n    when auth is disabled) get the unfiltered view; everyone else sees only\n    their own jobs.\"\"\"\n    if user is None or user.is_admin:\n        return None\n    return user.user_id"
}

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from core.node_data import build_node_data

    DOCUMENT_STAGE_DRIVER_SOURCE,
    DOCUMENT_STAGE_INDICATOR_SOURCE,
    DOCUMENT_VISIBILITY_REVIEW_ONLY,
    DOCUMENT_VISIBILITY_VISIBLE,
)

from packages.storage import NodeRecord, build_database_worker_storage_adapters, resolve_graph_store_paths
from research.question_domains import DomainGuess
from services.api import create_app
from services.api.config import ApiConfig
from services.worker.queue_runner import QueueWorkerRunner

    fake_research_runner as _fake_research_runner,
)

    import_seed_json_into_database as _import_seed_json_into_database,
)
from tests.support.api_runtime import (
    seed_repo_state as _seed_repo_state,
)


def test_graph_bundle_matches_frontend_shape(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _build_client(tmp_path)

    response = client.get("/api/graph/bundle")

    assert response.status_code == 200
    payload = response.json()
    assert set(payload.keys()) == {"nodes", "edges", "questions", "question_sets", "summaries"}
    assert payload["questions"][0]["id"] == "user_q_1"
    assert payload["question_sets"][0]["id"] == "local_set"
    assert "user_q_1" in payload["summaries"]

320	def test_jobs_and_artifacts_round_trip_through_api(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

326	    submit_response = client.post("/api/jobs/research", json={"question_ids": ["user_q_1"]})
327	    assert submit_response.status_code == 202
328	    accepted = submit_response.json()
329	    assert accepted["state"] == "queued"
330	    assert accepted["request_context"]["mode"] == "full_run"
331	
332	    worker_thread = threading.Thread(target=queue_runner.run_once, daemon=True)
333	    worker_thread.start()
334	
335	    running_seen = False
336	    status_payload = None
337	    for _ in range(40):
338	        status_response = client.get(accepted["status_url"])
339	        assert status_response.status_code == 200
340	        status_payload = status_response.json()
341	        if status_payload["state"] == "running":
342	            running_seen = True
343	            break
344	        time.sleep(0.02)
345	
346	    assert status_payload is not None
347	    assert running_seen
348	    assert status_payload["progress"]["total_count"] == 1
349	    assert status_payload["run_id"]
350	    assert status_payload["request_context"]["mode"] == "full_run"
351

354	        status_response = client.get(accepted["status_url"])
355	        status_payload = status_response.json()
356	        if status_payload["state"] in {"succeeded", "partial", "failed"}:
357	            result_response = client.get(accepted["result_url"])
358	            assert result_response.status_code == 200
359	            result_payload = result_response.json()
360	            break
361	        time.sleep(0.02)
362	
363	    worker_thread.join(timeout=2.0)
364	    assert result_payload is not None
365	    assert result_payload["job_id"] == accepted["job_id"]

28	def seed_repo_state(tmp_path: Path, *, frontend_dir: Path | None = None, api_key: str = TEST_API_KEY) -> ApiConfig:
29	    """Create a small deterministic repo-shaped workspace for API tests."""
30	    graphs_dir = tmp_path / "graphs"
31	    graphs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
32	    resolved_frontend_dir = frontend_dir or (tmp_path / "frontend")
33	    resolved_frontend_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
34	    output_root = tmp_path / "runtime" / "runs"
35	
36	    storage_runtime = StorageRuntimeConfig(
37	        database_url=f"sqlite+pysqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'storage.db').as_posix()}",
38	        local_workspace_root=tmp_path / "workspace",
39	        project_root=tmp_path,
40	    )
41	    adapters = build_database_worker_storage_adapters(storage_runtime)
42	    repo = adapters.graph_store_repository

User,\n    UserRegistry,\n)\nfrom services.api import create_app\nfrom tests.support.api_runtime import (\n    import_seed_json_into_database as _import_seed_json_into_database,\n)\nfrom \"alice@example.com\"\n\n\ndef _registry_with_two_users() -> UserRegistry:\n    sumeet = User(\n        user_id=\"sumeet\",\n        name=\"Sumeet\",\n        email=_SUMEET_EMAIL,\n        is_admin=True,\n    )\n    alice = User(\n        user_id=\"alice\",\n        name=\"Alice\",\n        email=_ALICE_EMAIL,\n        is_admin=False,\n        max_runs=5,\n    )\n    return UserRegistry(\n        by_email={_SUMEET_EMAIL: sumeet, _ALICE_EMAIL: alice},\n        defaults=RegistryDefaults(max_runs=3),\n    )\n\n\ndef _build_app(tmp_path: Path, registry: UserRegistry) -> TestClient:\n    config = _seed_repo_state(tmp_path, api_key=\"\")\n    _import_seed_json_into_database(config)\n    app = create_app(config=config)\n    app.state.user_registry = registry\n    return TestClient(app)\n\n\ndef _submit_as(client: TestClient, email: str) -> dict:\n    resp = client.post(\n        \"/api/jobs/research\",\n        json={\"question_ids\": [\"user_q_1\"]},\n        headers={CF_AUTHENTICATED_EMAIL_HEADER: email},\n    )\n    assert resp.status_code == 202, resp.text\n    return resp.json()\n\n\nclass TestSubmittedByPersisted:\n    def test_submit_stamps_resolved_user_id(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        client = _build_app(tmp_path, _registry_with_two_users())\n        accepted = _submit_as(client, _ALICE_EMAIL)\n        assert accepted[\"submitted_by\"] == \"alice\"\n\n    def test_status_response_surfaces_submitted_by(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        client = _build_app(tmp_path, _registry_with_two_users())\n        accepted = _submit_as(client, _ALICE_EMAIL)\n        status_response = client.get(\n            accepted[\"status_url\"],\n            headers={CF_AUTHENTICATED_EMAIL_HEADER: _ALICE_EMAIL},\n        )\n        assert status_response.status_code == 200\n        assert status_response.json()[\"submitted_by\"] == \"alice\"\n\n    def test_admin_submission_is_also_stamped(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        # Admins still get submitted_by set; the admin flag only affects\n        # the *list* view, not the *stamp* on their own jobs.\n        client = _build_app(tmp_path, _registry_with_two_users())\n        accepted = _submit_as(client, _SUMEET_EMAIL)\n        assert accepted[\"submitted_by\"] == \"sumeet\"\n\n    def test_unknown_cf_email_auto_provisions_and_stamps(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        # No matching TOML entry → auto-provisioned user with user_id = email.\n        client = _build_app(tmp_path, _registry_with_two_users())\n        accepted = _submit_as(client, \"newcomer@example.com\")\n        assert accepted[\"submitted_by\"] == \"newcomer@example.com\"\n\n\nclass TestListJobsFiltering:\n    def test_non_admin_sees_only_own_jobs(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        client = _build_app(tmp_path, _registry_with_two_users())\n        # Alice submits one, Sumeet submits one.\n \"newcomer@example.com\"},\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        payload = resp.json()\n        assert len(payload) == 1\n        assert payload[0][\"submitted_by\"] == {
  "command": "cd /Users/sumeet/matrix/elijah/.claude/worktrees/spi-246 && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nCross-FS: Stamp submitted_by + per-user job filtering (SPI-246)\n\nPersist the resolving user_id on every job row as submitted_by, and\nfilter the job list endpoints by submitted_by so each GUI user only\nsees their own jobs. Admins automatically see the unfiltered list —\nno separate toggle, the admin flag on the resolved User drives it.\n\nWires the get_current_user dependency into the three jobs routes\n(submit research, submit research step, list active, plus a new GET\n/jobs listing). The legacy require_api_key gate on the router-include\nstays untouched in this PR; SPI-247 will replace it wholesale.\n\nSchema:\n- New nullable submitted_by VARCHAR(255) column on jobs, indexed.\n- Alembic head bumped to 20260519_01.\n- JobAcceptedResponse and JobStatusResponse surface submitted_by so\n  the frontend can render the owner / filter the list.\n- DatabaseJobQueue.submit_job accepts submitted_by; new list_jobs\n  method returns recent jobs filtered by submitter (None = admin /\n  unfiltered).\n- JobService.list_jobs(submitted_by=...) wraps the queue and returns\n  list[JobStatusResponse].\n\nRoutes:\n- POST /api/jobs/research and /api/jobs/research-steps pick up the\n  current_user and pass submitted_by=user.user_id to the service.\n- New GET /api/jobs returns the current user's recent jobs (admins\n  see all).\n- GET /api/jobs/active now applies the same filter.\n\nBehavior is backward-compatible: anonymous calls (auth disabled in\ndev mode, or callers using the legacy shared ELIJAH_API_KEY which\nresolves to the fallback \"shared\" user) leave submitted_by as NULL\non the row. Existing jobs in deployed DBs predate the column and\nwill have NULL submitted_by, which the filter treats as \"no owner\"\nand correctly excludes from non-admin views.\n\nTests:\n- tests/test_api_submitted_by.py — 8 new tests covering submit\n  stamping (with CF-known, CF-admin, and auto-provisioned emails),\n  status response surfacing, and list filtering for admin vs.\n  non-admin vs. auto-provisioned users.\n- tests/test_fs5_odds_multiplier_head.py — alembic head sanity test\n  updated to the new head.\n\nPer the auth plan (docs/plans/auth-plan.md, decision #6 and the\nSPI-246 scope section), this is the second piece of the demo-auth\nfoundation. The run-cap ticket reads max_runs from the resolved\nUser and counts against submitted_by at submit time, building on\nthis PR.\n\nStacks on SPI-243 PR-2 (CF header bridging).\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -10",
  "description": "Commit SPI-246"
}
